# EveJS-only responses: canonical current-main audit

## Snapshot and scope

- Source checkout: `repository root`, branch `main`, start HEAD `52b8421f34e635900a18459da4403f26f2dfaec0`; final recheck HEAD `dda03ca0464043822e68c8348604ff34eadedcac`. Current registration counters include the parent dirty CmdAbandonLoot declaration read during final verification.
- Read-only references: `EveJS repository` at `828823a75f211fdecb945b087a26c225f111491f`; `eve-logic repository` at `1dbb5711a072a146e1d228365668ac05655eefe1` (V24.01).
- The checkout contains concurrent parent source/test changes. This is a source snapshot, not a build/runtime acceptance report. Core 698/Rust 835 passes are user-supplied parent results; this worker ran no build/test and made no Git/source/test mutations.
- CBM roots were checked as canonical absolute paths in the original source audit, where connected CBM succeeded. During this document reconciliation the MCP returned Transport closed; the documented CLI index_status confirmed repository root ready. No source/runtime acceptance was rerun. Serena recovery session was explicitly activated by absolute project path for each repository. Graph results were checked against live Service/Method attributes (109 service classes, 870 Method declarations), complete manual HasMethod tables, partial types, and concrete Host composition. Names alone are coverage, never evidence of equal responses.
- Existing `core-a-f.md`, `core-n-z.md`, `character-skills.md`, `corporations.md`, and `host-native-services.md` were used as leads. Their old findings are not accepted without current registration/provider checks.

## Counts and interpretation

| Coverage | Count | Meaning |
|---|---:|---|
| EveJS GetServiceInfo advertisement | 143 | Static routing dictionary, not the complete runtime registry |
| EveJS source registration candidates | 210 | Constructor/default names, thin re-exports and bootstrap aliases; constructor load failures were not executed |
| RS full Host profile registered keys | 181 | 177 primary names plus station/skillMgr/tradeMgr/standing2 aliases; PostgreSQL/SDE and space profile required |
| RS exported primary service/method pairs | 1212 | 870 attributed plus 342 manual HasMethod routes |
| RS exported service/method paths including aliases | 1280 | Additional 68 alias paths; bound OIDs are not separate services |
| Advertised EveJS names absent from RS | 11 | Presence difference only; publicGatewaySvc is client-local in V24.01 |
| All EveJS candidate names absent from RS | 29 | Includes non-advertised support/legacy services |
| Unique missing named RPC exports examined | 476 | 332 on present RS services plus 144 on absent services; inherited skillHandler and publicGatewaySvc duplicates removed |
| Gateway request namespace candidates | 182 | 179 deduplicated service selector names plus 3 inline producers; requests_pb2 aliases normalized; this is not 182 fully compared DTOs |

The 476 rows below confirm absent **literal named exits**, not 476 broken gameplay operations. Complete helper-return fields, remaining alternative semantic routes, live errors, and data-dependent behavior remain explicitly not confirmed. The revised core-a-f handoff establishes client-local coverage for three old names; it does not establish dynamic response parity. No row with an unknown DTO/body/caller is counted as response equality. Categories: **1** absent service, **2** absent export, **3** missing bound successor/binder, **4** incomplete result/container/fields, **5** missing notification, **6** session attributes/order, **7** unwired mutation/provider. Categories can overlap; do not add category counts as distinct RPC totals.

## Service boundary coverage (all 143 advertisements and every RS key)

Advertised but absent: `objectCaching, structure, loadService, planetOrbitalRegistryBroker, LSC, certificateMgr, tutorialSvc, bookmarkMgr, ownerGroupManager, contractMgr, publicGatewaySvc`.

Evidence for the complete boundary: `evejs-v0.12.9/server/src/services/machoNet/machoNetService.js:175`, `evejs-v0.12.9/server/bootstrap.js:187`, `../../../dotnet/src/Eve.Host/Hosting/HostComposition.cs:111`, `../../../dotnet/src/Eve.Host/Rpc/CharacterServiceList.cs:41`, `../../../dotnet/src/Eve.Services.Core/CoreServices.cs:10`. Main Host uses explicit instances, not a blanket generated catalog registration. A Service attribute or a generated catalog name does not register an instance.

## Unique EveJS-only named RPC ledger

Every row has its real EveJS handler path/line and the RS service type/table (or whole Host registration boundary for an absent service). “Other exit” names are discovery leads only, **not** proven substitutes. Error for a normal missing RS service is wrapped ServiceNotFound; missing method on a present service is wrapped AttributeError (`../../../dotnet/src/Eve.Services/PacketDispatcher.cs:122` and :150). Released bound OIDs instead receive UnMachoDestination. The EveJS None fallback is a deliberate reference difference, not a reason to synthesize success in RS.

| ID | Category | Service / RPC | Client operation / input | EveJS result type / fields | Current RS / classification | Other exit / alias | Both-side evidence | V24 callsite |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| EJS-0001 | 2 | account.GetAurumBalance | no arguments | EveJS returns a real-money value | Skipped: no direct V24 Python client callsite was found for this export | No RS handler is needed without a client route | EveJS accountService.js:775-778; V24 source search in eve/client/script | No direct V24 Python client callsite found |
| EJS-0002 | 2 | account.GetDefaultContactCost | no arguments | EveJS returns None | Skipped: the V24 account service reads the remote value into a client-local cache and maps None to -1; no server-owned contact cost exists | Client-local account service owns the default value | EveJS accountService.js:784-786; V24 accountsvc.py:151-153 and contactsForm.py:449 | Contact and email UI -> local account cache -> AccountMgr.GetDefaultContactCost |
| EJS-0003 | 2 | account.GetDefaultWalletDivision | no arguments | EveJS delegated return | Skipped: no direct V24 Python client callsite was found for this export | No RS handler is needed without a client route | EveJS accountService.js:780-782; V24 source search in eve/client/script | No direct V24 Python client callsite found |
| EJS-0004 | 2 | account.GetEntryTypes | no arguments | CachedMethodCallResult with 26 util.KeyVal rows: entryTypeID, entryTypeNameID, entryTypeName, entryJournalMessageID | Implemented with the EveJS V24 metadata mapping and ObjectCaching.MethodResult; the account client reads these rows when displaying journal entries | AccountService.GetEntryTypes returns the shared cached-method envelope | EveJS accountService.js:67-119,717-734; V24 accountsvc.py:97-101; regression checks row and envelope fields | Account client service -> AccountMgr.GetEntryTypes |
| EJS-0005 | 2 | account.GetJournal | accountKey, fromDate, rowCount, corp | EveJS returns a journal rowset | Skipped: no direct V24 Python client callsite was found for this export | No RS handler is needed without a client route | EveJS accountService.js:792-811; V24 source search in eve/client/script | No direct V24 Python client callsite found |

### Corrected old findings and intentional differences (excluded from missing-export counts)

- `tradeMgr -> trademgr` and `standing2 -> standingMgr` exist in current HostComposition:132-140. `station` and `skillMgr` also resolve to existing instances. These aliases must not be requested as new services.
- `CreateAlliance`, `GetAllianceApplications`, `GetSuggestedAllianceShortNames` are on the **CorpRegistryService partial type** in AllianceRegistryService.cs:1348, with the Service attribute on CorpRegistryService.cs:14 and Host CorporationRpc.cs:53. They are not misregistered alliance RPCs.
- AgentMissionService has MachoResolveObject and accepts every exported non-binder nested first call through HasMethod (AgentMissionService.cs:44,184-232). The old DoAction-only binder finding is withdrawn. The 16 remaining agent exports in the ledger are separate missing exits.
- dungeonInstanceCacheMgr.GetTriglavianSiteInstances is present in current HasMethod. The old A-F gap is withdrawn.
- Crate ClaimFitting uses the same PgSavedFittingStore as charFittingMgr (Persistence/Inventory/PersistenceCrateServiceBackend.cs:142-166, Rpc/InventoryRpc.cs:45, Rpc/FittingRpc.cs:25). The old JSON-only target finding is withdrawn.
- Current Office exports/remote-station operations were expanded by 009291c6. Old missing Office methods are not copied into this ledger; the notification question below is separate.
- Final readback found subsequent beyonce.CmdAbandonLoot (BeyonceService.cs:17-25) delegating to the registered EntityService authority after binding ownership. It is removed from missing-export counts. Full response/state/notification parity remains not confirmed; registration is not acceptance. Parent commit dda03ca0 changes Crate retry/reservation behavior; the old failed-claim-success candidate is not reintroduced from a stale report.
- shipCosmeticsMgr American-spelling aliases are absent, but V24 cosmeticsSvc.py:116,127 calls the registered British-spelling methods. This is an alias difference, not a demonstrated missing user response.
- ROLE_ADMIN/ROLE_SERVICE permission on machoNet.SetGlobalConfigValue agrees with CCP exported calls (eve-logic/carbon/common/script/net/machoNet.py:295-296). EveJS handler permits more. Retain the server role gate; no copying of permissive reference behavior is recommended.
- Host transactional atomicity for SKIN activation and dynamic-item creation is an intentional improvement recorded in host-native-services.md, not a missing response.
- Skills notification work is in progress; the Skills notification paths. The older character-skills.md notification/type findings are held as not confirmed until its current provider/announcer chain is reviewed; they are not duplicated as confirmed omissions here.
- GetPaperDollPortraitDataFor and IndustryMonitor.ConnectJob return native arrays in EveJS, but marshal.js:214-215 encodes those arrays as Python tuples. Their C# Py.Tuple return is therefore **not** a list/tuple gap. Other field/provider/access differences in host-native-services.md remain separate; its old container-only finding is withdrawn.

## Protobuf public gateway: separate transport, not a MachoNet alias

RS has no public gateway listener/Requests.Send/Notices.Consume producer in current production composition. The Rust↔Host link uses a custom binary frame and does not implement the public protobuf gateway. Adding `publicGatewaySvc` to ServiceInfo cannot service this transport. V24 PublicGatewaySvc is a **client-local service** (`eve-logic/publicGateway/publicGatewaySvc.py:25`), with requests and notice subscriptions at :142-211.

Historical intentional omission appears in TODO.md:854 and DEVIATIONS.md:3391; current TODO.md:56 explicitly reopens startup namespace/DTO gaps. Therefore these are recorded as **unimplemented gateway exits with an old intentional boundary**, not 182 proved gameplay defects and not response matches. Known reference compatibility stubs (campaign empty responses, saved-SKIN-capacity default payload) are identified; do not copy invented values or fake successful mutations.

EveJS wires 20 services in `evejs-v0.12.9/server/src/_secondary/express/gatewayServices/index.js:50`. Actual HTTP/2 gRPC routes are `evejs-v0.12.9/server/src/_secondary/express/publicGatewayLocal.js:2564`: Requests/Ping, Notices/Ping, Events/Ping, Requests/Send, Notices/Consume, Events/Publish. Request envelope fields are issued(Timestamp,1), correlation_uuid(bytes,2), payload(Any,3), external_origin(string,4), application_instance_uuid(bytes,5), authoritative_context(AuthoritativeContext,6); response fields are dispatched(Timestamp,1), correlation_uuid(bytes,2), status_code(uint32,3), status_message(string,4), payload(Any,5), internal_origin(string,6), application_instance_uuid(bytes,7), gateway_instance_uuid(bytes,8) (publicGatewayLocal.js:109-132). The producer uses `type.googleapis.com/` (publicGatewayLocal.js:1539), so the older spec's `type.evetech.net/` should not be treated as an observed local producer value. Exact application bytes, notice UUID/order and every nested DTO field are not runtime-verified.

## Remaining verification and handoff

- Full nested result fields and complete argument normalization are not compared for delegated helper returns in the 476 route rows; those cells stay not confirmed. A found raw array is a Python tuple in EveJS marshal.js:214-215; only an explicit type=list produces a Python list (:246-247). Do not retain the old “native array might be a list” ambiguity.
- Run equal-state EveJS/RS scenarios for the client-demonstrated missing map statistics, biomass, planet moniker and Agent journal/replay flows after implementation. IDs, callIDs, FILETIME timestamps, notification sequence numbers and generated UUIDs require normalization; state count, divisor, fields, errors and ordering are substantive.
- Gateway contract work needs descriptors, oneof/repeated/optional fields, authentication identity, statuses, response type URLs, notices and mutation owners. Replacing absent gateway responses with captured/default packets would not complete these paths.
- Serial builds and tests are handled by the test lane. Recommended verification: `dotnet test dotnet/tests/Eve.Host.Tests/Eve.Host.Tests.csproj --no-restore`, `dotnet test dotnet/tests/Eve.Services.Tests/Eve.Services.Tests.csproj --no-restore`, then affected feature suites and `cargo test -p eve-net`; this worker did not execute them. Actual installed graphical-client acceptance is separate.
- Artifact-only edits: this file and host-protobuf.md. No feature source, test, other worker report, settings, branch, commit, merge or push was changed by this worker.
